## 2026-11-20 — OWNER DIRECTION: NVP-POST-01 low-poly filming and LTX-2.5 presentation overhaul (ACTIVE; design documented, implementation OPEN)

Authority: [issue #162](https://github.com/vjmichael/nvp_offline_film/issues/162) and [design specification revision 2](https://github.com/vjmichael/nvp_offline_film/issues/162#issuecomment-7036120677). The owner now authorizes this redesign, superseding the November 15 deferral of production/post-production work, but not the single-main, existing-branch/PR, original-source preservation, security, acceptance, or independent main-merge requirements. Use existing draft PR #161 / nvp/mainline-convergence-20261113; no new branch or PR, no overlapping CI batches. Preserve all earlier accepted functionality and evidence; reconcile replacements through explicit behavioral adjudication, not deletion or waived tests.

**Binding product direction:** NVP creates data structures that produce a film, not a collection of video clips. Filming captures low-poly spatial/temporal performances, original dialogue, camera and source identities in immutable Takes (references to captured data), not photorealistic geometry or authoritative rendered frames. Encode/index/authenticate during capture; USDC is the selected candidate for USD layers, with time-indexed binary audio and other channels as appropriate; final storage/format migration remains gated by validated adapter requirements. Flatten only derived conditioning and presentation at the boundary. NVP owns original dialogue, voices and final soundtrack. LTX generates visuals; assume original sound is not preserved, normally discard generated output audio. Environmental sound, Foley, effects and music are independent sound-post assets. Native NVP-managed ltx-pipelines is the chosen integration, not a required ComfyUI application. FFmpeg/PyAV is permitted at the explicit external media boundary only, not the authoritative core; preserve core audit and scope/change inventory explicitly. Owner reports actual LTX-2.5 generations via ComfyUI on R9700; native protected worker/VRAM and merged-adapter behavior remain unqualified. Generative drift is accepted, with explicit filmmaker review/retry. No KAN/HDC, model-weight training or unreviewed migration.

### Journal update protocol

**Mandatory new-conversation first step:** reopen this master ledger and existing PR #161; do not create a new Git ref or PR. Every active update goes **here**, never in a new competing ledger: record `Task ID | exact candidate SHA | source PR/branch SHA | implemented changes | unmerged work | original run IDs/results | remaining acceptance | next actionable task`. Distinguish **source committed**, **test pending**, **native verified**, **physical verified**, and **merged to main**. Do not call a task DONE solely because a GitHub run is queued or a branch shares ancestry. Update branch rows only after measuring exact refs. Reconcile CI when results arrive, but immediately proceed on independent backlog work. Closing a draft PR is never the same thing as absorbing its functionality.

- Active consolidation candidate: `nvp/mainline-convergence-20261113` at snapshot `0490398e76fd1b1a81e00f2acdbfe56353f4158d`, created from PR #160's exact `0490398e76fd1b1a81e00f2acdbfe56353f4158d`. This candidate currently **contains the historic source sequence through #159 and #160 only**; other divergent lines are NOT merged.
- Enumerated branches: **209** total, including `main` and the temporary candidate. Enumerated open PRs: **139**. No branch/PR was silently marked integrated, verified, or retired solely because its older code *resembles* current code.
- Against `main`, PR #160's candidate is **2,357 commits ahead, 0 behind** (main is an ancestor), but includes many unaccepted native/installed/release gates. The audited HEAD alone is not production approval.
- The separate `nvp/w1g-p1a-sec06-integrated-20261112` security branch **diverges** from the P1-F candidate: 290 unique commits on W1-G versus 277 on PR #160, common ancestor `c1e5706d615108d05554946e0ffbb87588cc8135`. Neither branch is a safe automatic substitute for the other. The genuine W1-G/SEC-06 host-control/security work must be brought into the same candidate without discarding private worker restrictions.
- Other branches include parallel UI/G02/G03/G04–09, original USD-FILM/DA, production assets, native containment, sound, export, and historical design and FFmpeg-proxy paths; **each unique change requires explicit disposition**.

### Binding integration rules

1. **One release target:** `main`. The temporary candidate is a staging vehicle, not a competing perpetual mainline. All new NVP changes should land on or be promptly reconciled against the candidate. Avoid endless single-bug branch proliferation.
2. **Audit ALL branches:** for each exact SHA, compare to the candidate, inventory unique non-merge commits and touched interfaces, classify as (a) already present identically, (b) unique compatible changes to integrate, (c) superseded with named replacement tests and a cited equivalence proof, or (d) rejected/quarantined with a specific policy/security reason. Never claim completion because a branch name disappeared.
3. **Integration order:** authoritative data-first FilmIndex/OpenUSD/Take/PCM baseline; G02/G03 creator-facing UX and model boundary; native P1-A/W1-G/SEC-06 worker custody; G04–G09 filming/editing/export/actors/materials; P1-F body/face/terrain and genuine Cycles; provenance-preserving delivery; native Windows/Linux/installed release gate. The order can be interleaved when branches do not conflict.
4. **No regressions by consolidation:** retain original tests and original negative kernel cases, per-turn creator authorization, one-use SCM/AppContainer/WFP/Job Windows and root-owned delegated cgroup/namespace Linux custody. No debug/public fixture toggle in production code. No arbitrary executable/provider egress or private-source sandbox bypass.
5. **No legacy import by accident:** preserve the creator's active no-FFmpeg source policy. Old FFmpeg/proxy/unsafe test paths may need historical evidence and an explicit *superseded* disposition rather than blindly copying executables or workflows into main.
6. **Evidence gate:** a consolidated exact source SHA must pass full native Windows/Linux matrix, independent process-custody/guardian-loss/cancellation/restart/replay tests, original FilmIndex/source receipts, genuine licensed Blender/Cycles and audio verification where relevant, with separate physical and installed gates. A successful small public fixture does not mean whole-film or production installation acceptance.
7. **Merge protocol:** integrate changes into candidate via reviewed commits or real merges; resolve conflicts in code/tests rather than taking an arbitrary side. Run exact-head baseline. Create/maintain one **draft candidate → main** PR, mark ready and merge only after evidence and required explicit creator/release approvals. Retire superseded draft PRs/branches *after* ancestry/content review, never preemptively.
8. **Reconcile drift:** this snapshot must be refreshed when branch refs, open PRs or `main` advance. Never assume a parent PR's old pinned SHA reflects the latest compatible source automatically.

## Appendix B — Historical engineering journal and superseded task order (preserved verbatim except authority notices)

### Historical six-step NVP plan (archived; superseded by live CONV queue)

This order **supersedes older execution-order recommendations elsewhere in this historical ledger**, without erasing previous task evidence. Implementation may advance on independent workstreams while GitHub Actions runs; the corresponding acceptance/release gates remain strict. Technical implementation history is preserved in the P1-F asset notes; **that file is not an active task ledger**.

1. **Repair the demonstrated original-shot test mismatch** and finish all four Windows/Linux native combinations, including the genuine Cycles picture/audio test.
2. **Finish professional scene assets and performance**, using the reviewed material bindings already implemented.
3. **Run sustained rendering across genuine reel boundaries**, validating uninterrupted picture/sound delivery and adversarial recovery.
4. **Complete creator-facing filming, editing and live A/V**, including physical recording and spatial sound where hardware validation is required.
5. **Produce the 30-second film, then the actual 30-minute film**, before attempting the three-hour target.
6. **Complete packaging and release acceptance** only after the required independent evidence passes.

**Active Step 1 evidence (source head before repair `bdd653ceeb8769a3590b25db0c5dd135be3257e8`):** [Windows public native Cycles run #43555255623](https://github.com/vjmichael/nvp_offline_film/actions/runs/43555255623) and Linux 3.11/3.13 [P1-D/E run #43555255586](https://github.com/vjmichael/nvp_offline_film/actions/runs/43555255586) both exposed `test_cross_scene_nonmatching_take_and_tampered_original_png_refused` accepting a second material look ID for the same original scene/environment/material revision. The unchanged original assertion requires denial. Refuse equivalent active binding aliases under the FilmIndex writer lock; preserve separate genuinely distinct look/source scopes. At the same head, genuine Windows Blender preview UV/PBR source tests passed individually before the combined-shot suite was blocked by this original test; no two-shot Cycles/picture/WAV acceptance may be inferred. Required Linux root-owned OS custody, Windows SCM kernel and public broker tests passed separately; real installed private creator-signature acceptance remains OPEN.

**Release decision:** draft only; no merge or production installation, no complete 30-second/30-minute/three-hour film accepted.

**Step 2 active while Step 1 CI runs (2026-11-13):** reviewed rigged GLB character source now connects legacy PROD-06C bounded self-contained GLB inspection to the authoritative data-first FilmIndex actor asset/ActorID with durable original GLB SHA, explicit joint map, rest-pose calibration, creator-scoped rights evidence, and separately approved original scene casting. Per-frame evidence independently reopens source GLB, USD, scene, Take and source-time facial expression. No retargeted full-body deform, licensing verification, private Blender approval, actual professional assets or finished film accepted. `backend/nvp_p1f_production_character_assets.py`; original Windows/Linux test `tests/test_nvp_p1f_production_character_assets_native.py`. Exact current-head acceptance pending. Step 1's previously demonstrated source-look alias repair remains a separate native gate.

## PLAN-02-C — canonical CAM-FX rail / genuine local GGUF integration (source implementation)

**Authoritative path:** PR #74, canonical `nvp/data-av01-native-scene-playback-20261106`. Adapt bounded CAM-FX-01 rail interpolation and CAM-FX-02 separate director-owned USD camera layer, but **do not merge** diverged Film01 world-bank stack. `CanonicalSourceRails` binds an approved/frozen Digital Take SHA, independently checked OpenUSD stage, exact `ExactTimeShotSequence` camera timeline SHA/revision, valid authored original camera path, and a single gap-free camera shot window. Two to 48 strictly increasing integer original Film Time controls drive bounded speed/acceleration/easing, focal length, aperture/focus and USD source timecode samples. Stronger virtual camera USDA stored as its own verified presentation revision; original scene, ActorIDs, PCM and source camera are untouched. Source digest, predecessor, generated layer and current FilmIndex take/time are checked on **preview**, **before OS consent**, **after OS consent**, and **independent reopen**. No original 2D frame grid or renderer worker is created.

**Trusted creator authority:** first-party pywebview origin-only `plan02c_catalog/preview/publish/models/model_candidate` calls. Browser cannot set approval flags, model URLs, OS paths or tool commands. A fresh native OS confirmation is required to run a selected local model, and a distinct new OS confirmation is required to publish the exact rail preview. Source and one-use publication are CAS/revision and SHA guarded. All generated advice is unapproved until the creator edits/reviews it and performs the second operation.

**Actual model transport:** selectively port WO6 `ProvenLocalModelWriter`; NVP's existing `writer_for` discovers an already configured LOOPBACK provider, rejects redirects/proxies/model switching and bounds prompts, grammar, generated text and tokens. `generate_source_camera_advice` prompts with *actual* OpenUSD source tick observations and verified Take SHA; 2–4 candidate nodes must preserve exact first/end integer Film Time, pass full rail physical bounds, source recheck and a matching positive provider completion receipt. This is only **provider-response evidence**, not a GGUF weight attestation and never tool authority. Optional real smoke `tools/nvp_plan02c_real_gguf.py` launches genuine llama.cpp on a separately hashed, fixed SHA-256 official Qwen2.5 0.5B Q4_K_M GGUF, compares server+weights hashes, verifies a completed unapproved camera response and unchanged original Take/PCM; run only in the **marked group-end** single DATA-AV grouped acceptance job.

**No arbitrary merger:** old Film01 ActorID camera-bank service, 01M layer, Cam-FX protected renderer, source editing Gizmo/dragging, independently accepted 7B+ Windows Radeon performance, physical human native GUI interaction and cinema-quality shot frames remain separate. PLAN-02-D protected renderer/recovery, PLAN-02-E 30s FFmpeg-free film, PLAN-02-F installed physical and consolidation are not claimed complete.

**Acceptance state:** pending exact-head grouped Windows/Linux 3.11/3.13 native OpenUSD rail and hostile approval/AI contract tests; React director controls/build; bundled Windows installer; genuine hashed local Qwen/llama.cpp completion in separate **job within that one grouped run**. Fake-provider contract tests are never counted as actual GGUF output. Record real failures and fix *before* claiming accepted PLAN-02-C.

Imported only host-owned original PCM spool/recovery, miniaudio mono capture, independently calibrated two-device ADC clock mapping and dual PortAudio capture, with their genuine native failure/denial/cancellation tests. **Kept** PR #74's newer `NativeSceneSoundCapture`, immutable FilmIndex publications, source verification, OpenUSD, and exact Film Time implementation unchanged.

The trusted installed WebView2 bridge now provides an exact source camera/timeline catalog, OS-scoped microphone/device selection, separate native permission dialogs for recording and Take-freeze, interruption inventory, and native cancellation. The native 3D A/V React panel offers source-bound original microphone capture; no JS field can assert human consent or prescribe an input file or process. PyInstaller includes these explicit adapters, including sounddevice PortAudio, with **zero FFmpeg**. Existing device-clock-led scene audition remains the playback source; film-picture rendering and advanced HRTF quality are not established by this donor port.

**Acceptance pending:** consolidated DATA-AV-01 Windows/Linux Python 3.11/3.13, React and NSIS installed candidate must execute on the integrated exact head and genuine failures must be repaired. Synthetic native device tests cannot establish installed OS microphone permission, independent hardware loopback/drift, real audiovisual latency, or professional sync. PLAN-02-C (camera and human-reviewed GGUF) follows only after the native task group's defects are resolved. Legacy source branch CI results are historical evidence, **not acceptance** of this integrated head.

editing, playback, media import and final mastering must be DATA-FIRST;

discovery, executable, CLI, decoder, exporter or test prerequisite. Old
source code may remain in historical commits, but **must not be an active
product path**. Reusable third-party components are welcome provided they

performance and exact signed-64-bit Film Time events (7,056,000,000
ticks/second), Digital Take hashes, source-signal references and

and visuals as requested. Only an explicit final 2D presentation freezes
output raster/video/audio; samples/frames never define a Digital Take.

### 3. Historical execution order — superseded by the authoritative six-step sequence at the top

1. **P0: Close active native defects and source divergence.** First repair P1-B `makensis` path from original Windows log; finish new Windows installed-candidate run. Reconcile source from #156 on latest #74 (including the original PCM/OS authority and updated legacy recorder cleanup); repair demonstrated Windows E2E-01D and USD-02C exact-time failures. Keep physical ADC/EFS gates open without blocking unrelated source implementation. Do not repeatedly launch GitHub jobs just to watch queues.
2. **P1: DA-00 + DA-06 authority cleanup; DA-01/DA-02 missing source/editor seams.** Audit the actual app and bundle for reachable legacy/FFmpeg execution. Finish source-immutable exact-time editor/ActorID/scene/Take controls and correct source identity/capture recovery. PLAN-02-C (camera rails + human-reviewed local GGUF) follows PLAN-02-B in the **existing** integration map; reconcile its source tests/GUI, no independent model grant.
3. **P2: DA-03 physical capture as a separate hardware lane; DA-04 live A/V as the main functional lane.** Finish real two-ADC device timing/acoustics on suitable Windows hardware while implementing PLAN-02-D source-selected protected renderer and continuous audio-clock-led viewport/acoustic engine. Required 30-second 3D test: real authored scene, actors, two camera viewpoints, moving/occluded sound emitter, continuous picture/audio, bounded seek/cancel/restart receipts.
4. **P3: DA-05 / PLAN-02-E final-film path.** Complete native no-FFmpeg import/export, protected color/geometry/PCM delivery, independently inspect the 30-second multi-shot end product and original Take immutability. Carry long-session and professional-delivery dependencies in backlog instead of substituting old frame/proxy exports.
5. **P4: DA-07 / PLAN-02-F one-source release gate.** After P0–P3 functionality, consolidate exact Windows/Linux native and installed acceptance, dependency signatures/worker provisioning, physical EFS recovery, separate creator-authorized NSIS upgrade+rollback, uninterrupted 30-minute filming and later feature-length endurance. Any failed source/installed/human gate remains explicitly OPEN.

**Release decision:** Not ready to integrate into `main`, install as an authorized production upgrade, or declare DA-00–DA-07 complete. Advance the engineering sequence without blocking source work on hosted CI, but never promote a queued/failed or different-SHA result into acceptance.

## Migration rules

- No claim of complete migration while an active UI/API path still
  produces an authoritative `frame_count`/`start_frame` or generates
  a legacy proxy for native filming or review.
- No deletion of immutable existing source data or silent rewriting of
  previously captured projects. Old formats can be inspected/imported
  only through a separately marked migration reader.
- Prefer one shared evaluator for the same Film Time/scene function.
  Preserve first-party authorization, opaque bounded windows, SHA
  provenance and recovery; tests cannot be waived merely because
  GitHub runs are delayed.
- All work and repair receipts go into
  `docs/NVP_ENGINEERING_STATE.md` and this checklist. The live branch
  is PR #74, `nvp/data-av01-native-scene-playback-20261106`.

## Work performed and outstanding defects

The existing USD/TemporalLedger and DATA-AV-01 source chunk reader,
dry spatial PCM mixer, native sound bridge and audio-clock-linked
world-state inspector establish the data-first foundation, **not**
complete acceptance. The most recent independently observed native
CI at commit `06546564941b319ccea436be59427d213db480ab`
failed: spatial USD time-code type, native consent test assumptions,
one existing React test; Windows installed acceptance was skipped.
These are tasks to repair, not releases to approve.

**Zero-FFmpeg clarification:** prior statements that FFmpeg could
remain for external media or final export are explicitly withdrawn.
A decoder/encoder must come from another suitable library or a native
format adapter; where none exists, the operation remains unavailable
until implemented instead of silently invoking FFmpeg.

### 2026-11-07 DA-07 Windows GUI-process exit-code repair
At exact inspected PR #74 head `7c0a14dc2e8551f13dd032f18fb5a58f6d211473`, DATA-AV run [42329066286](https://github.com/vjmichael/nvp_offline_film/actions/runs/42329066286) completed with DA-00 zero-FFmpeg audit, four Windows/Linux native OpenUSD/PCM Python 3.11/3.13 jobs, and React **passed**; installed Windows job **failed**. Its log explicitly showed `Installed native Digital Take and sound playback failed (exit )` (empty exit value), not a demonstrated nonzero Python exit. The workflow invoked a **windowed** PyInstaller NVP.exe using `&` then checked `$LASTEXITCODE`, which can be unset for GUI processes. Commit `4bd8c60224d05a8ed03e86d13999f255e306021d` replaces both packaged and installed smoke calls with `Start-Process -Wait -PassThru` and checks actual `.ExitCode`; diagnostics remain available. This fixes an observable CI harness defect, but the smoke result and installed WebView2/uninstall checks on the **new** head remain unverified until CI finishes. Do not treat prior empty `$LASTEXITCODE` as evidence of an application crash, and do not claim installed acceptance. DA-00 through DA-07 overall acceptance remains OPEN; continuous 3D, real audio device sync, advanced acoustics/HRTF, filming capture and approved 3D-to-2D delivery remain independently unverified. No FFmpeg invocation or fallback added.
